The remaining funds of $2,500 is **payable upon the conclusion** of your event *(phải trả khi kết thúc)*. In case of **cancellation** *(việc huỷ)*, the deposit is **non-refundable** *(không hoàn lại)*. The total amount of $5,000 is **inclusive of** a $500 **service fee** *(đã bao gồm / phí phục vụ)*.

> ⚠️ Lớp text bản đề làm hỏng dòng tiêu đề của phiếu (đọc thành `mmmii`), địa chỉ e-mail (`homesteaclprop.com`) và giờ sự kiện (`7 P.m.-10:00 P.M.`). Đã khôi phục theo bản dịch chính thức (KEY trang 25: "행사 예약 확인: **Grand-Mont 호텔**", "오후 7시-오후 10시") và theo địa chỉ đúng xuất hiện ở phiếu.

**191.** What is the main purpose of the letter?
- (A) ✓ To confirm an intention to **resign from** a position *(từ chức khỏi)*
- (B) To **turn down** an offer for an **executive** job *(từ chối / cấp điều hành)*
- (C) To notify an executive of a **contract extension** *(việc gia hạn hợp đồng)*
- (D) To request some **time off** for personal reasons *(thời gian nghỉ)*

> 📘 Đoạn 1: `This letter serves as **official notification of my intention to step down** as the company's vice president of finance` ⇒ thư chính thức báo việc **từ nhiệm** ⇒ (A). Hợp đồng của ông **hết hạn**, không phải được gia hạn (loại C).

**192.** According to the e-mail, what will Ms. Ingram be offered?
- (A) A printed invitation for a party
- (B) ✓ A **monetary incentive** *(khoản thưởng bằng tiền)*
- (C) A **complimentary** meal *(miễn phí)*
- (D) A copy of an **agreement** *(thoả thuận, hợp đồng)*

> 📘 Cuối e-mail: `the company is happy to offer you **an extra $500 on your next paycheck as compensation**` → khoản tiền thưởng thêm ⇒ (B). Món quà mà `The board and I will take care of` là dành cho **ông Grainger**, không phải cho bà Ingram.

**193.** What did Ms. Montessori ask Ms. Ingram to do?
- (A) Arrange a corporate awards ceremony
- (B) ✓ Plan a **retirement celebration** *(tiệc mừng nghỉ hưu)*
- (C) Purchase a gift for an employee
- (D) **Recruit** volunteers to **decorate a venue** *(tuyển / trang trí địa điểm)*

> 💡 Ghép **thư + e-mail**: thư của ông Grainger báo ông **nghỉ hưu**; e-mail viết `I would like to organize a **dinner party in his honor**` và nhờ bà `take care of the arrangements` ⇒ bà được nhờ tổ chức **tiệc chia tay nghỉ hưu** ⇒ (B). Tiệc trao giải là việc bà đã làm **mùa thu năm ngoái** (loại A); quà thì `The board and I will take care of` (loại C).

**194.** What can be inferred about Ms. Ingram?
- (A) She reserved two rooms for the event.
- (B) She invited fewer guests than expected.
- (C) She needs to **submit a deposit** to the venue soon *(nộp tiền cọc)*.
- (D) ✓ She booked an event venue **under budget** *(dưới mức ngân sách)*.

> 💡 Ghép **e-mail + phiếu xác nhận**: e-mail cho hạn mức `We plan to spend **up to $6,000** on the party's location`; phiếu ghi `The total amount of **$5,000**` ⇒ bà đặt địa điểm **rẻ hơn ngân sách** ⇒ (D). (C) sai vì phiếu đã xác nhận `receipt of your $2,500 **deposit**` — tiền cọc **đã nộp**; (B) ngược: dự kiến 50–60 khách nhưng phiếu ghi **66** khách.

**195.** What has Ms. Ingram requested from the Grand-Mont Hotel?
- (A) An **invoice** for the total cost *(hoá đơn)*
- (B) Some recommendations for menus
- (C) ✓ Some **audiovisual equipment** *(thiết bị nghe nhìn)*
- (D) A choice of two meal options

> 📘 Dòng `**SPECIAL REQUESTS**` trong phiếu ghi `**Microphone and projection system with sound**` → chính là thiết bị nghe nhìn ⇒ (C). Bẫy số lượng: bữa ăn có **ba** lựa chọn (beef, fish, vegetarian), không phải hai (loại D).

### Questions 196–200 · advertisement, text message, and notice

> http://www.portkeyclassifieds.com/realestate/rentals
> HOME | **CLASSIFIED ADS** *(rao vặt)* | POST AN AD | CONTACT
>
> **DOWNTOWN APARTMENT FOR RENT: 543 Mapleton Street**
>
> This 950-**square-foot** apartment *(bộ vuông, ~0,093 m²)* is located in the city center in Portsmouth Residential Towers. It contains two bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen and dining area, a bathroom, and a **balcony** *(ban công)*. The **residence** is conveniently located *(nơi ở, căn hộ)* near a bus stop, a subway station, and a shopping mall. The **unit** *(căn hộ)* comes with a space in the building's **parking garage** *(nhà để xe)*, the cost of which is included in the $1,900 monthly **rental fee** *(tiền thuê)*. New **wallpaper** *(giấy dán tường)* will be installed for the new **resident** *(người ở, cư dân)*. The apartment will be ready to move into as soon as this work is finished on August 30. **Contracts** are for one or two years *(hợp đồng)* with the possibility of an **extension** *(gia hạn)*. A one-month **security deposit** *(tiền đặt cọc)* is required along with a **reference** from a previous **landlord** *(thư/người bảo chứng / chủ nhà cũ)*. To **book a viewing** *(hẹn xem nhà)*, please contact George Lee at georgelee@vendorrealty.com, or call him at 555-0393.

> From: Neil Kaplan (555-3004)
> To: George Lee (555-0393)
> Received: May 25, 4:10 p.m.
>
> Thank you for showing me the three apartment units this morning. I'm interested in renting the one on Mapleton Street. I understand that the owner needs a **reference prior to signing any agreement** *(thư bảo chứng trước khi ký hợp đồng)*. He is free to contact Miranda Chase at mirandach@verymail.com. She's the **proprietor** of the **studio apartment** *(chủ sở hữu / căn hộ một phòng)* I currently rent in Tanner Hill Condominiums. I am prepared to sign a two-year contract and move into the unit as soon as the new wallpaper has been **put up** *(dán, lắp lên)*. Thank you.

> **NOTICE: All Tenants** *(người thuê nhà)*
>
> Tanner Hill Condominiums' underground parking facility will **undergo resurfacing** this week *(được làm lại mặt nền)*. The work will begin on Monday, May 30, and last until Friday. The facility will be **inaccessible** *(không vào được)* not only during this time but also on the weekend as the concrete requires time to **set properly** *(đông cứng đúng cách)*. Tenants may once again park their vehicles in the garage on Monday, June 6.
>
> Tenants should move their vehicles **one day before the work begins**, to the Downtown Parkade located across the road at 168 Beauregard Avenue. Simply pick up a **temporary pass** *(thẻ tạm)* from the facility's **ticket office** *(phòng vé)*, and **display** it on your vehicle's **windshield** *(trưng, đặt / kính chắn gió)*.
>
> We apologize for any inconvenience and **disturbance** the work may cause *(sự phiền nhiễu)*. For more details, contact the building's **administrative office** *(văn phòng hành chính)* at 555-4958 during its **operating hours** of 8 a.m. to 4 p.m. *(giờ làm việc)*, from Monday to Friday.

**196.** What is included in the advertised apartment's rental fee?
- (A) A security system
- (B) ✓ A parking spot
- (C) **Maintenance** costs *(bảo trì)*
- (D) Electricity charges

> 📘 Quảng cáo: `The unit comes with **a space in the building's parking garage**, the cost of which is **included in the $1,900 monthly rental fee**` ⇒ tiền thuê đã gồm **chỗ đỗ xe** ⇒ (B).

**197.** What is implied about Mr. Kaplan?
- (A) He should pay the rent by **bank transfer** *(chuyển khoản)*.
- (B) He must **renew** a contract after one year *(gia hạn)*.
- (C) He will not be **refunded** a deposit *(hoàn lại)*.
- (D) ✓ He can move into a unit from August 30.

> 💡 Ghép **quảng cáo + tin nhắn**: quảng cáo ghi `The apartment will be **ready to move into as soon as this work** [dán giấy tường] **is finished on August 30**`; tin nhắn của ông Kaplan nói `I am prepared to ... **move into the unit as soon as the new wallpaper has been put up**` ⇒ ông có thể dọn vào **từ 30/8** ⇒ (D). Ông chọn hợp đồng **hai năm**, nên không phải gia hạn sau một năm (loại B).

**198.** Who is Miranda Chase?
- (A) ✓ The owner of a **residential unit** *(căn hộ để ở)*
- (B) A representative of a **real estate agency** *(công ty môi giới bất động sản)*
- (C) The **administrator** of Portsmouth Residential Towers *(người quản lý)*
- (D) A tenant of a Mapleton Street apartment

> 📘 Tin nhắn: `She's the **proprietor of the studio apartment I currently rent** in Tanner Hill Condominiums` → bà là **chủ căn hộ** mà ông Kaplan đang thuê ⇒ (A). Người làm môi giới là **George Lee** (loại B); ông Kaplan mới là người **sắp** thuê căn ở Mapleton Street (loại D).

**199.** What will Mr. Kaplan probably have to do on May 29?
- (A) ✓ Park his vehicle at the Downtown Parkade
- (B) Move his **belongings** into an apartment *(đồ đạc)*
- (C) Meet with a landlord to sign an agreement
- (D) Purchase a pass at a ticketing office

> 💡 Ghép **tin nhắn + thông báo**: tin nhắn cho biết ông Kaplan **hiện đang thuê nhà ở Tanner Hill Condominiums**, tức là một `tenant` mà thông báo nhắm tới; thông báo yêu cầu `Tenants should move their vehicles **one day before the work begins** [work begins **Monday, May 30**], to the **Downtown Parkade**` → một ngày trước 30/5 là **29/5** ⇒ ông phải đưa xe sang bãi Downtown Parkade ⇒ (A). Thẻ đỗ xe là `**temporary pass**` chỉ cần **đến lấy** (`pick up`), không nói phải mua (loại D).

**200.** What is indicated about Tanner Hill Condominiums?
- (A) It currently has no **vacant units** *(căn trống)*.
- (B) It charges monthly fees for parking.
- (C) It is **situated nearby public transit** *(nằm gần phương tiện công cộng)*.
- (D) ✓ It closes its office on weekends.

> 📘 Câu "indicated". Cuối thông báo: văn phòng hành chính làm việc `from **8 a.m. to 4 p.m., from Monday to Friday**` → **không** làm việc cuối tuần ⇒ (D). (C) là đặc điểm của **Portsmouth Residential Towers** trong quảng cáo (gần bến xe buýt, ga tàu điện ngầm), không phải Tanner Hill — bẫy nhầm toà nhà.

## TEST 04

### Questions 147–148 · invitation

> **The Committee for World Education Development**
> would like to **cordially invite** you to *(trân trọng kính mời)*
> **The 20th Annual Portland Study Abroad Fair**
> January 10 through 14, from 10 a.m. to 4 p.m.
> Barring Exhibition Hall, 475 Wharton Drive
> Portland, Oregon
>
> The Committee for World Education Development is proud to **host** this event *(đăng cai, tổ chức)* in downtown Portland. The fair will involve more than 2,000 **representatives** *(đại diện)* from universities, colleges, language schools, and other **academic institutions** *(cơ sở học thuật, trường học)* from around the globe.
>
> Join us on any day of the fair by showing this card at the **registration desk** *(bàn đăng ký)* near the front entrance of the exhibition hall. The invitation **entitles the recipient to** bring one additional guest *(cho phép người nhận được quyền)*.

**147.** What type of event is the invitation for?
- (A) A city's **tourism promotion** fair *(quảng bá du lịch)*
- (B) A conference on international languages
- (C) An organization's student awards ceremony
- (D) ✓ An **exposition** for **overseas** education *(hội chợ triển lãm / ở nước ngoài)*

> 📘 Tên sự kiện là `The 20th Annual Portland **Study Abroad** Fair` (hội chợ **du học**), quy tụ `representatives from universities, colleges, language schools ... **from around the globe**` ⇒ đây là triển lãm về **giáo dục ở nước ngoài** ⇒ (D).

**148.** How can the invitation's recipient attend the event?
- (A) ✓ By **presenting** the card near a hall entrance *(xuất trình)*
- (B) By making a **donation** to a **scholastic institution** *(khoản quyên góp / cơ sở giáo dục)*
- (C) By providing the committee with **confirmation in advance** *(xác nhận trước)*
- (D) By showing an **identity card** at the registration desk *(chứng minh thư)*

> 📘 Đoạn cuối: `Join us on any day of the fair by **showing this card at the registration desk near the front entrance**` → chỉ cần xuất trình **tấm thiệp mời** ở bàn đăng ký gần cửa vào ⇒ (A). Bẫy (D) đổi "thiệp mời" thành "chứng minh thư"; không có yêu cầu báo trước (loại C).

### Questions 149–150 · notice

> **Space Science Museum**
> **NOTICE TO VISITORS**
>
> Please note that the main building of the Space Science Museum at 290 Chamomile Street will be closed from June 4 to June 8 for scheduled **restoration work** *(công việc tu sửa)*.
>
> The work will improve the **foundation** and **interior design** *(nền móng / thiết kế nội thất)* of a number of **exhibition halls** *(gian triển lãm)* to better **enhance** museum experiences *(nâng cao)* for our guests. The museum's **planetarium** will also be closed *(nhà chiếu hình vũ trụ)*, as staff will be **installing** new equipment *(lắp đặt)* and **upgrading** seats *(nâng cấp)*.
>
> However, the museum's **auxiliary buildings** *(các toà nhà phụ)* will remain open during this period. Visitors with **passes** to the special exhibition *(vé, thẻ vào cửa)*, *The Moons of Saturn*, may still **access** it *(vào, tiếp cận)* at Orbit Hall. **Likewise** *(tương tự)*, those wishing to visit our space transport display may do so at Satellite Hall.
>
> All museum **facilities** will officially reopen *(cơ sở, hạng mục)* on Thursday, June 9. We apologize for the **inconvenience** *(sự bất tiện)*.

**149.** What is the notice mainly about?
- (A) Major exhibitions for the month
- (B) ✓ **Temporary facility closures** *(việc đóng cửa tạm thời)*
- (C) Scientific research programs
- (D) Museum foundation events

**150.** What is stated about the museum?
- (A) Its interior design work will last for a month.
- (B) Its entrance locations will be temporarily changed.
- (C) ✓ Some of its **venues** will be open to the public during construction *(địa điểm, khu vực)*.
- (D) It **postponed** *The Moons of Saturn* exhibition *(hoãn)*.

> 📘 Câu "stated". Đoạn 3: `However, the museum's **auxiliary buildings will remain open** during this period` — và nêu cụ thể Orbit Hall, Satellite Hall vẫn đón khách ⇒ (C). (A) sai thời lượng: chỉ từ **4/6 đến 8/6** (năm ngày); (D) ngược lại — triển lãm *The Moons of Saturn* **vẫn xem được** ở Orbit Hall.

### Questions 151–152 · e-mail

> To: Joseph Hillier <j.hillier@romanos.com>
> From: Marilyn Sharpe <m.sharpe@romanos.com>
> Subject: Burton Farms
> Date: October 21
> Attachment: Product list
>
> Joseph,
>
> As you know, we are in the process of **negotiating an agreement** *(đàm phán một thoả thuận)* with Burton Farms to sell some of their **dairy products** *(sản phẩm từ sữa)* at all our supermarket locations in the Midwest. Depending on how well the products sell, we may **distribute** them later on *(phân phối)* to our other stores across the country.
>
> **In connection with this** *(liên quan đến việc này)*, I'd like your team to complete a presentation **in time for** a meeting *(kịp cho)* we are having with Burton Farms on the 11th of next month. You will need to do some research on their **brand positioning** and **target market** *(định vị thương hiệu / thị trường mục tiêu)*. Next, you'll have to **come up with** ideas *(nghĩ ra)* for arranging the products on our **shelves** *(kệ hàng)* and **promoting** them to our customers *(quảng bá)*. I've attached the Burton Farms product list and **highlighted** the items we plan to sell *(đánh dấu nổi bật)*. I'll be in the office all week if you want to discuss this further.
>
> Thanks!
> Marilyn Sharpe
> Vice president of marketing
> Romano's Groceries

**151.** According to Ms. Sharpe, what does Romano's Groceries plan to do?
- (A) **Take over** the operation of Burton Farms *(tiếp quản)*
- (B) Open further **distribution centers** in the Midwest *(trung tâm phân phối)*
- (C) Hold training sessions for its staff members
- (D) ✓ **Enter into a supply arrangement** with a **dairy** *(ký thoả thuận cung ứng / cơ sở sản xuất sữa)*

> 📘 Câu đầu: `we are in the process of **negotiating an agreement with Burton Farms to sell some of their dairy products**` → sắp ký thoả thuận **mua bán/cung ứng** với một nhà sản xuất sữa ⇒ (D). Họ chỉ **bán hàng** của Burton Farms chứ không mua lại công ty đó (loại A).

**152.** What has Mr. Hillier's team NOT been asked to do?
- (A) Finish a presentation for a meeting
- (B) Perform some **market studies** *(nghiên cứu thị trường)*
- (C) ✓ List dairy items to be sold
- (D) Develop concepts for **product displays** *(cách trưng bày sản phẩm)*

> 📘 Câu NOT — đối chiếu các việc bà giao ở đoạn 2: (A) có ở `complete a **presentation** in time for a meeting`; (B) có ở `do some **research** on their brand positioning and target market`; (D) có ở `come up with ideas for **arranging the products on our shelves**`. Riêng danh mục hàng thì **chính bà Sharpe đã làm sẵn** — `**I've attached** the Burton Farms product list and **highlighted the items we plan to sell**` ⇒ nhóm không phải lập danh sách ⇒ (C).

### Questions 153–155 · announcement

> The Millsboro Water Department is **calling on** all area students *(kêu gọi)* to participate in its third annual art contest. The **theme** of this year's competition *(chủ đề)* is *Water For All*.
>
> The contest is open to Millsboro students in grades 4 through 12. The rules call for **original artwork** *(tác phẩm gốc, tự sáng tác)* to be submitted on 75-centimeter by 110-centimeter **poster board** *(bìa cứng làm áp phích)* or paper, with a 3-centimeter **border** on all sides *(lề, viền)*. **Entrants** *(người dự thi)* may use any traditional **medium** they wish *(chất liệu, phương tiện thể hiện)*, such as **pastel crayons** *(bút sáp màu pastel)*, colored pencils, **watercolor paints** *(màu nước)*, cut paper, or **fabric** *(vải)*. However, **computer-generated** artwork *(do máy tính tạo ra)* and photography will not be accepted. Students may receive **guidance** from a parent, teacher, or **guardian** *(sự hướng dẫn / người giám hộ)* but must do all the work by themselves.
>
> The **grand prize** winner *(giải nhất, giải lớn)* of each grade level will receive $300 cash and a $200 **gift card** from fellow **sponsor** Millsboro Shopping Center *(phiếu quà tặng / nhà tài trợ)*. Furthermore, three additional **contestants** *(thí sinh)* will be awarded with $100 gift cards, also **furnished by** the shopping center *(do ... cung cấp)*. Along with other **entries** *(bài dự thi)*, the winning **pieces** *(tác phẩm)* will be **featured** in next year's student artwork calendar *(được đưa vào, giới thiệu)*, which will be **distributed** free *(phát)* to clients of the Millsboro Water Department at the beginning of next year.
>
> The **deadline** for entries *(hạn chót)* is April 30. For more information, visit www.mwd.com/art_contest.

**153.** What is the purpose of the announcement?
- (A) ✓ To **publicize** a local event *(quảng bá, thông báo rộng rãi)*
- (B) To introduce a public service
- (C) To promote water **conservation** *(việc tiết kiệm, bảo tồn)*
- (D) To inform residents about a change

**154.** What is NOT indicated about the Millsboro Water Department?
- (A) It requires entries to have specific **measurements** *(kích thước)*.
- (B) It will not accept **submissions** produced on a computer *(bài nộp)*.
- (C) ✓ It released a series of posters on water usage last year.
- (D) It is **cosponsoring** an event with Millsboro Shopping Center *(đồng tài trợ)*.

> 📘 Câu NOT — đối chiếu: (A) có ở `**75-centimeter by 110-centimeter** poster board ... with a 3-centimeter border`; (B) có ở `**computer-generated artwork** ... will not be accepted`; (D) có ở `a $200 gift card from **fellow sponsor** Millsboro Shopping Center`. Còn việc **phát hành áp phích năm ngoái** thì hoàn toàn không được nhắc ⇒ (C) — thông báo chỉ nói đây là cuộc thi **lần thứ ba**.

**155.** What will the Millsboro Water Department do the next year?
- (A) Sponsor student art **exhibits** at area schools *(triển lãm)*
- (B) Sell some artworks to **raise funds for charity** *(gây quỹ từ thiện)*
- (C) **Expand** its services to other locations *(mở rộng)*
- (D) ✓ **Hand out** calendars with student art to its clients *(phát)*

> 📘 Đoạn 3: các tác phẩm đoạt giải `will be featured in **next year's student artwork calendar**, which will be **distributed free to clients** of the Millsboro Water Department **at the beginning of next year**` ⇒ (D).

### Questions 156–157 · text message chain

> **Dawn Konrad** [4:48 p.m.]
> Yoon-Hee, are you busy tomorrow? Ms. Jackson sent a message saying that we need to **rush** the Lambert project *(làm gấp)*. I'll be **working overtime** on it *(làm thêm giờ)* tomorrow night and **could use your help** *(rất cần bạn giúp)*.
>
> **Yoon-Hee Sung** [4:51 p.m.]
> I'm **heading to** the marketing conference tomorrow *(sắp đi tới)*, remember? I'll be **out of town** for two days *(đi công tác xa)*.
>
> **Dawn Konrad** [4:52 p.m.]
> Oh, that's right. It completely **slipped my mind** *(tôi quên khuấy mất)*. Should I ask Mr. Martin?
>
> **Yoon-Hee Sung** [4:54 p.m.]
> I'd try Mr. Stewart. He's worked on the project longer and knows Percy Lambert really well.
>
> **Dawn Konrad** [5:00 p.m.]
> I'll do that. Thanks, and have a good trip!

**156.** What is true about Ms. Konrad?
- (A) She will attend a marketing conference.
- (B) She will be **assigned to** a new project *(được phân công vào)*.
- (C) ✓ She will be working extra hours tomorrow.
- (D) She will ask Ms. Jackson for help.

> 📘 Câu "true". Tin 4:48 p.m. của chính bà Konrad: `I'll be **working overtime** on it **tomorrow night**` ⇒ (C). Người đi dự hội nghị marketing là **bà Sung** (loại A); bà Jackson là người **giao việc gấp**, không phải người được nhờ giúp (loại D).

**157.** At 4:54 p.m., what does Ms. Sung most likely mean when she writes, "I'd try Mr. Stewart"?
- (A) She believes he can suggest a **replacement** *(người thay thế)*.
- (B) She has already tried to contact Mr. Martin.
- (C) ✓ She thinks he is more **qualified** than Mr. Martin *(đủ năng lực, phù hợp)*.
- (D) She is not **well acquainted with** Percy Lambert *(quen biết rõ)*.

> 📘 Câu hỏi ý định. Bà Konrad vừa hỏi `Should I ask **Mr. Martin**?`; bà Sung đáp "Tôi thì tôi sẽ hỏi **Mr. Stewart**" rồi nêu **lý do**: `He's worked on the project **longer** and **knows Percy Lambert really well**` → bà cho rằng ông Stewart **phù hợp hơn** ông Martin ⇒ (C).

### Questions 158–160 · advertisement

> **Send Yourself To The Next Level**
>
> Whether you're a professional **athlete** *(vận động viên)* or just an **enthusiastic amateur** *(người nghiệp dư đầy nhiệt huyết)*, having the right shoe is **crucial** for improving your **performance** *(thiết yếu / thành tích, khả năng thể hiện)*. The Lynx **sneaker** *(giày thể thao)* is the result of 25 years of research and **experimentation** *(thử nghiệm)* into the perfect athletic shoe. The Jet Shoe Company has worked with some of the finest sports scientists and **conducted tests** *(tiến hành thử nghiệm)* with the world's top athletes to develop the Lynx. And the effort shows in everything you do while wearing them.
>
> Our **patented** Absorbex technology *(đã được cấp bằng sáng chế)* **cushions your joints from impacts** *(đệm bảo vệ khớp khỏi lực va chạm)* as you run while providing a firm surface to **spring off from** *(bật lên từ đó)*. And the **collar** is specially designed *(cổ giày)* to provide extra **stability** to your **ankle** *(sự vững chắc / mắt cá chân)*, reducing the risk of **sprains** *(bong gân)*. The Lynx is used by top athletes like **sprinter** Dwayne Harrison *(vận động viên chạy nước rút)* and basketball player Andrew Singer. Top **coach** Dennis Potts *(huấn luyện viên)* of the Baltimore Crabs football team recommends all his players wear shoes that use the technology when in the gym.
>
> The Lynx is available at most general sports **retailers** *(nhà bán lẻ)*. To find your nearest **outlet** *(cửa hàng)*, check our Web site at www.jetshoe.com.

**158.** The word "performance" in paragraph 1, line 3, is closest in meaning to
- (A) **production** *(sự sản xuất)*
- (B) ✓ **ability** *(khả năng, năng lực thể hiện)*
- (C) **exhibition** *(cuộc trưng bày)*
- (D) **fulfillment** *(sự hoàn thành, thoả nguyện)*

> 📘 Câu từ vựng theo ngữ cảnh: `having the right shoe is crucial for **improving your performance**` — nói về **thành tích/khả năng vận động** của người mang giày ⇒ `ability`. (C) là nghĩa "buổi biểu diễn" của `performance` — đúng từ nhưng sai ngữ cảnh thể thao này.

**159.** What does the advertisement mention about the Lynx sneaker?
- (A) ✓ It protects the **joints** while in use *(khớp)*.
- (B) It is the cheapest training shoe available.
- (C) It supplies additional warmth in winter.
- (D) It is sold in a wide variety of colors.

> 📘 Đoạn 2: `Our patented Absorbex technology **cushions your joints from impacts** as you run` → công nghệ này **bảo vệ khớp** khi chạy ⇒ (A). Quảng cáo không hề nói về giá, màu sắc hay khả năng giữ ấm.

**160.** Where can customers purchase the advertised product?
- (A) In some gym facilities
- (B) At an upcoming event
- (C) On the **manufacturer's** Web site *(nhà sản xuất)*
- (D) ✓ At athletic goods shop

> 📘 Đoạn cuối: `The Lynx is **available at most general sports retailers**. To find your nearest outlet, **check our Web site**` → bán tại các **cửa hàng đồ thể thao**; trang web chỉ để **tra cứu địa chỉ cửa hàng**, không phải nơi bán ⇒ (D), loại (C). Phòng gym chỉ là nơi huấn luyện viên khuyên **mang** giày, không phải nơi bán (loại A).
>
> *(Sách in phương án (D) là `At athletic goods shop` — thiếu mạo từ/số nhiều; đây là lỗi của chính bản in, đã đối chiếu ảnh trang 101 nên giữ nguyên.)*

### Questions 161–163 · letter

> **WINSPEAR HOTEL**
> 3498 Ada Boulevard, Edmonton AB T6A 2C1
>
> Paul Mansbridge
> 2405 Cottonwood Lane
> Windsor, ON N9B 3P4
>
> November 14
>
> Dear Mr. Mansbridge,
>
> Thank you for the letter dated November 12 regarding your stay at the Winspear Hotel in Edmonton. — [1] —. I understand that you and a **colleague** were our guests *(đồng nghiệp)* from November 8 to 10 but had some issues during your visit.
>
> The **manager on duty** *(quản lý trực ca)*, Melanie Depuis, has **notified** me *(báo cho)* that one of the rooms you had reserved was **double-booked** *(bị đặt trùng cho hai khách)*. — [2] —. Unfortunately, we could not correct our mistake after your arrival since the hotel was at **full occupancy** *(kín phòng)*. We are very sorry, and **enclosed** are two **vouchers** *(gửi kèm / phiếu ưu đãi)* for a **complimentary** one-night stay *(miễn phí)* at any Winspear Hotel **nationwide** *(trên toàn quốc)* — one for you and one for your **associate** *(cộng sự)*, David Solomon.
>
> You also said you thought our buffet breakfasts were complimentary. **In regard to this** *(về việc này)*, morning meals are only free for those staying in our **premium accommodations** *(phòng hạng sang)*. — [3] —. As you and Mr. Solomon stayed in a **standard room**, we had to **charge** you for your breakfasts *(tính tiền)*. I apologize for any **misunderstanding** *(sự hiểu lầm)*.
>
> — [4] —.
>
> We value the continued **patronage** of our guests *(sự ủng hộ, lui tới thường xuyên)*, and we hope the vouchers will **make up for** any inconvenience *(bù đắp cho)*.
>
> Sincerely yours,
> *(chữ ký tay)* Kimberley Bell
> General manager, Winspear Hotel Edmonton

**161.** Why did Mr. Mansbridge most likely send a letter on November 12?
- (A) To confirm booking details
- (B) To **inquire about** an extra room *(hỏi về)*
- (C) To ask about a travel program
- (D) ✓ To make a **complaint** about a stay *(lời phàn nàn)*

> 📘 Câu suy luận. Thư trả lời mở đầu `Thank you for the letter dated **November 12** regarding your stay ... but **had some issues** during your visit`, rồi lần lượt xin lỗi về **phòng bị đặt trùng** và **tiền ăn sáng** ⇒ thư ngày 12/11 của ông là **thư khiếu nại** ⇒ (D).

**162.** What is true about Mr. Mansbridge?
- (A) He was charged extra for requesting an additional bed.
- (B) His **membership points** were not **credited** correctly *(điểm thành viên / được cộng vào)*.
- (C) He will receive a **complete refund** for his stay *(hoàn tiền toàn bộ)*.
- (D) ✓ He was given vouchers for a hotel stay.

> 📘 Câu "true". Đoạn 2: `enclosed are **two vouchers for a complimentary one-night stay** at any Winspear Hotel nationwide — **one for you** and one for your associate` ⇒ (D). Khách sạn tặng phiếu nghỉ, **không** hoàn tiền (loại C); khoản bị tính thêm là **bữa sáng**, không phải giường phụ (loại A).

**163.** In which of the positions marked [1], [2], [3], and [4] does the following sentence best belong?
"As a result, you were forced to share a room with your traveling companion."
- (A) [1]
- (B) ✓ [2]
- (C) [3]
- (D) [4]

> 📘 Câu chèn câu. `**As a result**` đòi hỏi câu trước nêu **nguyên nhân**, và `share a room` chỉ hợp lý khi thiếu phòng. Câu ngay trước [2] cho biết `one of the rooms you had reserved was **double-booked**` → hệ quả là hai người phải **ở chung một phòng** ⇒ [2]; câu tiếp sau cũng khớp (`we could not correct our mistake ... the hotel was at full occupancy`). Ở [3], [4] mạch đã chuyển sang chuyện bữa sáng và lời cảm ơn.

### Questions 164–167 · letter

> **Pure Mix**
> PO Box 12525
> Oregon City, OR 97045
>
> Paul McCall
> 898 Manatee Lane
> Madison, WI 53716
>
> Dear Mr. McCall,
>
> We have received your **inquiry** about our natural daily vitamin **supplement** *(thắc mắc / thực phẩm bổ sung)*. You asked if our product contained any chemicals or **nonorganic materials** *(nguyên liệu không hữu cơ)*. Also, you inquired about some of the **ingredients** *(thành phần, nguyên liệu)* used to make the vitamin powder, as you are **allergic to** strawberries *(dị ứng với)* and cannot **ingest** grapefruit *(nạp vào cơ thể, ăn/uống)* for medical reasons.
>
> As advertised, we are a provider of **certified organic** products *(hữu cơ được chứng nhận)*, so there are no chemical or **unnatural** materials *(không tự nhiên, nhân tạo)* in our products at all. Our organic ingredients are of the highest quality available, made only from fruits, plants, and **naturally raised animals** *(vật nuôi theo cách tự nhiên)*.
>
> As stated, our products do include fruits. However, the daily vitamin supplement does not contain strawberries or grapefruit as ingredients. You can ingest it safely without any worry about an **allergic reaction** *(phản ứng dị ứng)* to strawberries or **medication interaction** *(tương tác thuốc)* with grapefruit. But some of our other products may contain one or the other. For example, Pure Mix energy drinks contain **grapefruit seed** *(hạt bưởi chùm)*.
>
> I hope I have provided **satisfactory** responses *(thoả đáng)* to your questions. Enclosed, you'll find an **informative brochure** *(tờ gấp nhiều thông tin)* on our most popular products. As always, should you plan to start a regular vitamin **regimen** *(chế độ dùng đều đặn)*, we highly recommend that you first **consult** your **physician** *(hỏi ý kiến / bác sĩ)*.
>
> Sincerely,
> Linda Mills
> Customer relations manager

**164.** Why was the letter written?
- (A) To place a vitamin order
- (B) To obtain organic **certification** *(chứng nhận)*
- (C) To inquire about an advertisement
- (D) ✓ To respond to a **prior inquiry** *(thắc mắc trước đó)*

> 📘 Câu mở đầu: `**We have received your inquiry** about our natural daily vitamin supplement`, và cả thư lần lượt **trả lời** từng câu hỏi của ông McCall ⇒ (D). Chính ông McCall mới là người hỏi (loại C).

**165.** What does Ms. Mills say about Pure Mix's products?
- (A) They all have strawberries as ingredients.
- (B) They do not contain any animal materials.
- (C) ✓ They are all completely organic.
- (D) They have yet to be released on the market.

> 📘 Đoạn 2: `we are a provider of **certified organic products**, so there are **no chemical or unnatural materials in our products at all**` ⇒ (C). (B) trái với `made only from fruits, plants, and **naturally raised animals**`; (A) trái với đoạn 3 (thực phẩm bổ sung **không** chứa dâu tây).

**166.** Why should Mr. McCall avoid drinking Pure Mix's energy drinks?
- (A) They will cause him to have an allergic reaction.
- (B) ✓ They have an ingredient he cannot **consume** *(dùng, nạp vào)*.
- (C) They are **prohibited for diabetics** *(bị cấm với người tiểu đường)*.
- (D) They will give him **digestion issues** *(vấn đề tiêu hoá)*.

> 💡 Cần ghép **hai chi tiết trong thư**: đoạn 1 nói ông `cannot ingest **grapefruit** for **medical reasons**`; đoạn 3 cho biết `Pure Mix **energy drinks contain grapefruit seed**` ⇒ nước tăng lực có thành phần ông không được dùng ⇒ (B). Lưu ý bẫy (A): thứ gây **dị ứng** cho ông là **dâu tây**, còn bưởi chùm là vấn đề **tương tác thuốc** — không phải dị ứng.

**167.** What does Ms. Mills suggest Mr. McCall do?
- (A) Research the benefits of natural medications
- (B) ✓ Speak to a doctor before taking **dietary supplements** *(thực phẩm chức năng)*
- (C) Order vitamins from a manufacturer's Web site
- (D) Keep the product's package after opening it

> 📘 Đoạn cuối: `should you plan to start a regular vitamin regimen, we highly recommend that you **first consult your physician**` → hỏi ý kiến **bác sĩ** trước khi dùng ⇒ (B).

### Questions 168–171 · article

> **A Very Fruity Idea**
>
> Our cities are filled with trees, and the **vast majority** of those trees *(đại đa số)* produce **edible** fruits *(ăn được)*. — [1] —. But with no one to collect and **process** them *(chế biến)*, most simply fall to the ground and **rot** *(thối rữa)*, wasting a **potentially** excellent source of **nutrition** *(tiềm tàng / dinh dưỡng)*.
>
> One new organization wants to change that. — [2] —. Fresh From The Tree is a **non-profit group** *(tổ chức phi lợi nhuận)* that **aims to** collect as much fruit as possible *(hướng tới việc)* from the city's trees, and then make it into delicious snacks that can be sold at low prices to local residents.
>
> "We have been doing a **survey** of the locations *(cuộc khảo sát)* of all the fruit trees in town," says **co-founder** Michaela Anderson *(đồng sáng lập)*. "Once we have **identified** a **promising** tree *(xác định được / có triển vọng)*, we contact the **homeowners** *(chủ nhà)* and ask if we can pick the fruit for them. — [3] —. They usually don't have time to manage it themselves."
>
> After the **volunteers** collect the fruit *(tình nguyện viên)*, they give half of it to the owners of the **residence** *(nhà ở)*, and keep the other half to turn into fruit pies, **jams**, **sorbets** *(mứt / kem trái cây)*, and other tasty but healthy **desserts** *(món tráng miệng)*. The group only has **a handful of** volunteers *(một số ít)* at the moment but are hoping to **recruit** more *(tuyển thêm)* in the coming months. — [4] —. They are also **negotiating with** the city *(thương lượng với)* to collect fruit from trees on **government property** *(đất công)*.

**168.** What is the article mainly about?
- (A) The **establishment** of a firm *(việc thành lập)*
- (B) The opening of a fruit **orchard** *(vườn cây ăn quả)*
- (C) The management of public land
- (D) ✓ The work of an organization

**169.** What does Fresh From The Tree do with the fruit it collects?
- (A) Sells it to local restaurants
- (B) ✓ Turns it into snacks
- (C) **Donates** it to a charity *(quyên tặng)*
- (D) **Transports** it to City Hall *(vận chuyển)*

> 📘 Đoạn 2: nhóm `aims to collect as much fruit as possible ... and then **make it into delicious snacks**`, và đoạn 4 nói rõ họ giữ một nửa `to turn into fruit pies, jams, sorbets, and other ... desserts` ⇒ (B). Họ bán trực tiếp cho **dân cư địa phương** với giá thấp, không bán cho nhà hàng (loại A).

**170.** According to the article, what do homeowners receive from Fresh From The Tree?
- (A) A small payment for the food
- (B) ✓ Some of the collected **produce** *(nông sản, hoa quả thu hoạch)*
- (C) **Recognition** at a public event *(sự ghi nhận, vinh danh)*
- (D) Free **baked goods** *(đồ nướng, bánh trái)*

> 📘 Đoạn 4: `After the volunteers collect the fruit, they **give half of it to the owners of the residence**` → chủ nhà nhận lại **một nửa số quả** ⇒ (B). Nhóm không trả tiền, và phần bánh trái làm ra là để **bán** (loại A, D).

**171.** In which of the positions marked [1], [2], [3], and [4] does the following sentence best belong?
"Most of the residents accept the offer with pleasure."
- (A) [1]
- (B) [2]
- (C) ✓ [3]
- (D) [4]

> 📘 Câu chèn câu. `the **offer**` phải trỏ về một lời đề nghị vừa được nêu, và `the **residents**` phải đã xuất hiện. Câu ngay trước [3] là `we contact the **homeowners** and **ask if we can pick the fruit for them**` — chính là lời đề nghị ⇒ [3]; câu tiếp sau (`They usually **don't have time** to manage it themselves`) giải thích **vì sao** họ vui vẻ đồng ý. Ở [1], [2] chưa có lời đề nghị nào.

### Questions 172–175 · online chat

> **Cheryl Stone** [3:48]
> I just got a message from Mr. Baker saying that his flight has been **delayed** until 9 p.m. *(bị hoãn)* Weather conditions are terrible there in Chicago.
>
> **Allan Franklin** [3:50]
> Really? But he's **supposed to** make a presentation *(lẽ ra phải)* to the **executives** from Harper Telecom *(ban lãnh đạo)* today!
>
> **Maurice Wood** [3:51]
> I've got all the **visuals** ready *(hình ảnh minh hoạ, slide)*. But it would be **problematic** *(rắc rối, thành vấn đề)* if we don't have a speaker.
>
> **Allan Franklin** [3:52]
> That's **an understatement** *(nói thế còn là nhẹ)*. We need this contract. Otherwise, we may not **meet our sales target** for the year *(đạt chỉ tiêu doanh số)*.
>
> **Cheryl Stone** [3:54]
> Can't we do the presentation ourselves?
>
> **Allan Franklin** [3:55]
> I'm not sure. There are several firms **competing for** the contract *(cạnh tranh giành)* we are trying to get. Harper Telecom could decide to give the contract to someone else if they aren't **impressed with** our presentation *(ấn tượng với)*.
>
> **Cheryl Stone** [3:57]
> I think I could do the speaking, but without Mr. Baker's **technical background and expertise** *(nền tảng kỹ thuật và chuyên môn)*, our **proposal** might not sound very **convincing** *(đề xuất / thuyết phục)*. What do you think?
>
> **Allan Franklin** [3:58]
> I agree with you. I think it would be better to call Harper Telecom and try to **convince** them *(thuyết phục)* to listen to his **pitch** *(bài chào hàng, bài thuyết trình chào mời)* tomorrow morning. That might be our best option.
>
> **Cheryl Stone** [3:59]
> I'm on it. I'll let you know how it goes.

> ⚠️ Lớp text OCR của bản scan cắt mất từ cuối câu `we may not meet our sales target for the ...`; đã phục hồi thành `for the **year**` theo bản dịch chính thức (KEY trang 31: "올해의 매출 목표").

**172.** What is suggested about the presentation?
- (A) It targets a company based in Chicago.
- (B) It is scheduled for tomorrow morning.
- (C) It is going to be uploaded to a Web site.
- (D) ✓ It is aimed at **securing a contract** *(giành được hợp đồng)*.

> 📘 Câu suy luận. Lúc 3:52 `We need **this contract**`, và 3:55 `There are several firms **competing for the contract** we are trying to get. Harper Telecom could decide to give the contract to someone else if they aren't impressed with **our presentation**` ⇒ buổi thuyết trình nhằm **giành hợp đồng** ⇒ (D). Buổi sáng mai mới chỉ là **điều họ định xin**, chưa chốt (loại B); Chicago là nơi **ông Baker đang kẹt**, không phải trụ sở Harper Telecom (loại A).

**173.** What can be inferred about Harper Telecom?
- (A) They are **unsatisfied with** a presentation *(không hài lòng với)*.
- (B) ✓ They are considering **offers** from several businesses *(đề nghị, chào giá)*.
- (C) They want Mr. Baker to participate in the presentation.
- (D) They will not be available at a different time.

> 📘 Câu suy luận. Lúc 3:55: `There are **several firms competing for the contract** we are trying to get` → Harper Telecom đang cân nhắc đề xuất của **nhiều công ty** ⇒ (B). Buổi thuyết trình **còn chưa diễn ra** nên chưa thể hài lòng hay không (loại A); việc họ có rảnh sáng mai hay không thì nhóm mới **định gọi hỏi** (loại D).

**174.** What is mentioned about Mr. Baker?
- (A) He is preparing to go to Chicago on business.
- (B) ✓ He is **proficient in** technical matters *(thành thạo, giỏi về)*.
- (C) He previously worked at Harper Telecom.
- (D) He produced all of the presentation materials.

> 📘 Lúc 3:57 bà Stone nói `without **Mr. Baker's technical background and expertise**, our proposal might not sound very convincing` → ông có **chuyên môn kỹ thuật** ⇒ (B). Ông đang **kẹt ở Chicago để bay về**, không phải đang chuẩn bị đi (loại A); người chuẩn bị slide là **ông Wood** (loại D).

**175.** At 3:59, what does Ms. Stone mean when she writes, "I'm on it"?
- (A) ✓ She will call Harper Telecom.
- (B) She will give a presentation tomorrow.
- (C) She will wait for Mr. Baker's flight to arrive.
- (D) She will contact her **absent** colleague *(vắng mặt)*.

> 📘 Câu hỏi ý định. Câu ngay trước của ông Franklin: `it would be better to **call Harper Telecom** and try to convince them to listen to his pitch tomorrow morning` → "Tôi lo ngay đây" = bà sẽ **gọi cho Harper Telecom** ⇒ (A). Người sẽ thuyết trình sáng mai là **ông Baker** (loại B).

### Questions 176–180 · notice and letter

> **CITY OF GRIGGSTON PUBLIC MEETING NOTICE**
>
> A city hall meeting has been scheduled to discuss **proposed plans** *(kế hoạch được đề xuất)* for two road improvements, specifically the **extension** of Marigold Street *(việc kéo dài)* from Route 103 and the **road hazard repairs** *(sửa chữa các điểm nguy hiểm trên đường)* on Hartford Street. The first project on Marigold Street will be **conducted** over two weeks *(tiến hành)* while the **latter** is expected to take five days *(cái sau, dự án sau)*. The Griggston Office of Transportation Planning has provided **funding** for both projects *(kinh phí)*.
>
> The work is expected to cause some **disruption** to traffic *(sự gián đoạn)* and some **disturbances** to homes and businesses *(sự phiền nhiễu)* in the area. The city has already **put together** a **traffic rerouting plan** *(soạn ra / kế hoạch phân luồng lại giao thông)* for the **duration** of both projects *(thời gian diễn ra)* and will allow the usage of noisy **machinery** *(máy móc)* during daytime hours.
>
> Please note the meeting details below.
>
> | | |
> |---|---|
> | **Date:** | Tuesday, January 10 |
> | **Time:** | 6:30 p.m. |
> | **Location:** | Griggston City Hall, Room 5A in basement level one |
>
> This room is **fully-accessible for persons with disabilities** *(hoàn toàn thuận tiện cho người khuyết tật)*.
>
> **Representatives** from the Office of Transportation Planning *(đại diện)*, along with project **contractors** and **consultants** *(nhà thầu / chuyên gia tư vấn)*, will present an **overview** of the roadwork *(cái nhìn tổng quan)* while providing the opportunity for questions from the **audience** *(người dự)*. Should you have any **inquiries** about this meeting *(thắc mắc)* or comments regarding either project, they may be **directed to** our senior city planner *(gửi tới)* Lois Oliveira at l.oliveira@griggstoncity.gov.

> **Griggston City Hall**
> 100 West Lorry Road
> Griggston, TX 76645
> Office of Transportation Planning
>
> February 15
>
> Shawn Davis
> Walnut Creek Contracting
> 58 Lilac Terrace
> Fort Worth, TX 76120
>
> Dear Mr. Davis,
>
> I am sending this letter as an expression of my **gratitude** *(lòng biết ơn)* for the excellent work your **contracting company** *(công ty nhà thầu)* did on the Marigold Street construction work. You and your 10 **crew members** *(nhân công, thành viên tổ thi công)* were a pleasure to work with and were always **timely, professional, and knowledgeable** *(đúng hẹn, chuyên nghiệp và am hiểu)*.
>
> Already, there have been a lot of positive reactions about the work you've done from residents and business owners **in the vicinity** *(ở khu vực lân cận)*. There have been many positive **remarks** *(nhận xét)* about the beautiful **brick sidewalks** *(vỉa hè lát gạch)* that were installed.
>
> This was now the fifth time for us to hire your company and I am delighted that your business and the City of Griggston have continued our **ongoing partnership** *(quan hệ hợp tác lâu dài)*. I have **wired the payment** *(chuyển khoản thanh toán)* for your **contracted services** *(dịch vụ theo hợp đồng)* to your company's bank account **as per our regular agreement** *(theo đúng thoả thuận thường lệ)*.
>
> Also, I would like to let you know that my office is working on a similar project for Rosedale Avenue. Nothing is **confirmed as of yet** *(chưa được chốt)*, but I should have more details in a few weeks. I would also like to consider your company for this task. It would take **approximately** the same amount of time *(khoảng chừng)* as the work on Marigold Street did and would possibly begin at the end of April. Let me know if you might be interested.
>
> Thank you again, and we look forward to working with you in the future.
>
> Sincerely,
> Lois Oliveira
> Senior city planner

**176.** What is indicated about the City of Griggston?
- (A) It has city hall meetings once every month.
- (B) ✓ It had some issues with its road conditions.
- (C) It has hired contractors for building **renovations** *(cải tạo)*.
- (D) It will increase **property taxes** to pay for upcoming projects *(thuế nhà đất)*.

> 📘 Câu "indicated". Thông báo nêu hai dự án đường, trong đó có `the **road hazard repairs** on Hartford Street` (sửa các điểm **nguy hiểm** trên mặt đường) ⇒ thành phố có vấn đề về tình trạng đường sá ⇒ (B). Kinh phí đến từ `the Griggston Office of Transportation Planning`, không nói gì tới việc tăng thuế (loại D).

**177.** What does the notice state about the city's projects?
- (A) They will not create much noise.
- (B) They will end three weeks after a city council meeting.
- (C) ✓ They will cause traffic to be **detoured** *(bị chuyển hướng, đi vòng)*.
- (D) They will be voted on by the public.

> 📘 Câu "state". Đoạn 2: `The city has already put together a **traffic rerouting plan** for the duration of both projects` → giao thông sẽ được **phân luồng đi hướng khác** ⇒ (C). (A) trái với việc thành phố `will allow the usage of **noisy machinery**`; buổi họp chỉ để **trình bày và hỏi đáp**, không bỏ phiếu (loại D).

**178.** According to the notice, how can residents provide feedback on the roadwork projects?
- (A) By calling Lois Oliveira's personal **assistant** *(trợ lý)*
- (B) ✓ By sending an e-mail to a city **official** *(quan chức, cán bộ)*
- (C) By visiting project contractors
- (D) By posting on the city's online forum

> 📘 Cuối thông báo: `Should you have any inquiries ... or **comments regarding either project**, they may be directed to our **senior city planner Lois Oliveira at l.oliveira@griggstoncity.gov**` → gửi **e-mail** cho một cán bộ thành phố ⇒ (B).

**179.** What can be inferred about Mr. Davis?
- (A) He asked for extra money to cover **unexpected costs** *(chi phí phát sinh)*.
- (B) He suggested using bricks for the city's **pedestrian walkways** *(lối đi bộ)*.
- (C) He organized the transportation office's meeting.
- (D) ✓ He **supervised** a group of 10 workers on a project *(giám sát, phụ trách)*.

> 📘 Câu suy luận. Thư gửi cho ông Davis của **Walnut Creek Contracting** viết `**You and your 10 crew members** were a pleasure to work with` → ông đứng đầu một tổ **10 nhân công** ⇒ (D). Thư nói tiền được trả `**as per our regular agreement**` nên không có chuyện đòi thêm (loại A); vỉa hè gạch chỉ được **khen**, không nói ai đề xuất (loại B).

**180.** What is indicated about Walnut Creek Contracting?
- (A) It only accepts service payments by check.
- (B) It presented building plans during a recent public meeting.
- (C) It **submitted a contracting bid** for the Hartford Street project *(nộp hồ sơ dự thầu)*.
- (D) ✓ It may do some roadwork on Rosedale Avenue for two weeks.

> 💡 Ghép **thông báo + thư**: thông báo cho biết `The first project on **Marigold Street** will be conducted **over two weeks**`; thư nói dự án Rosedale Avenue `would take **approximately the same amount of time as the work on Marigold Street** did` và bà muốn `consider your company for this task` ⇒ công ty có thể sẽ làm đường Rosedale trong **khoảng hai tuần** ⇒ (D). Tiền được `**wired** ... to your company's **bank account**`, tức chuyển khoản chứ không phải séc (loại A).

### Questions 181–185 · brochure and e-mail

> Are the **countertops** in your home or workplace *(mặt bàn bếp, mặt quầy)* **worn or outdated** *(cũ mòn hoặc lỗi thời)*? Let Carlson Counters give your kitchen, office, bathroom, or **reception area** *(khu tiếp tân)* a brand-new look!
>
> We offer a **vast assortment** of styles, colors, and materials *(vô số chủng loại)* to choose from! And our prices are some of the best in town:
>
> | Material | Price |
> |---|---|
> | **Laminate** *(nhựa ép lớp)* | Starting at $80 per square meter |
> | **Tile** *(gạch men)* | Starting at $90 per square meter |
> | **Granite** *(đá hoa cương)* | Starting at $130 per square meter |
> | **Marble** *(đá cẩm thạch)* | Starting at $150 per square meter |
>
> All countertops listed above are available for **customization** at increased rates *(làm theo yêu cầu riêng)* if requested at least 15 days **in advance** *(trước)*. In such cases, Carlson Counters charges **$120 per square meter for laminate and tile** customization and **$190 per square meter for specialized orders in granite or marble**. Carlson Counters does require a 50 percent **deposit** for any custom order *(tiền đặt cọc)*.
>
> All you have to do is call us at 555-4933 or send an e-mail to rogcarl@carlsoncounters.com to request an **on-site visit** *(chuyến khảo sát tận nơi)*. Owner Roger Carlson will visit your space to get **rough measurements** *(số đo sơ bộ)* and discuss **installation options** *(phương án lắp đặt)* with you. He will also describe the various materials we sell and provide you with **cost estimates** *(bảng ước tính chi phí)* for the different countertops you can choose. Once you have made your selection, Mr. Carlson will immediately schedule the work and let you know how long it will take.
>
> So, contact Carlson Counters today and find out how we can give your space a new and **contemporary** look *(hiện đại, hợp thời)*!

> TO: Roger Carlson <rogcarl@carlsoncounters.com>
> FROM: Blaire Thomson <bomson@gotomail.com>
> SUBJECT: Countertops
> DATE: May 29, Thursday
>
> Dear Mr. Carlson,
>
> Thank you for coming by my home this afternoon to **check out** my kitchen *(xem xét)*. I appreciate the information you provided as it **confirmed** what your brochure said *(xác nhận, khớp với)*.
>
> I have discussed the materials you suggested with my husband. Both of the **recommendations** are attractive *(gợi ý)*, but after talking about it, we have agreed that we should **go ahead with** the marble option *(quyết định chọn, tiến hành với)* for our countertops. Please send us the total cost estimate for the project when you have time.
>
> You also mentioned during your visit that custom countertops like the one we're requesting need to be **specially ordered** *(đặt riêng)* and may require additional time for delivery. So, could you let us know how long it will take to arrive?
>
> I hope to hear from you soon, and thank you for your excellent service.
>
> Regards,
> Blaire Thomson

**181.** What is indicated about Carlson Counters?
- (A) It provides services for **residential** spaces only *(nhà ở)*.
- (B) ✓ It accepts visit requests through multiple **channels** *(kênh, cách thức)*.
- (C) It is **situated** in a central part of the town *(toạ lạc)*.
- (D) It is currently offering a service at a discount.

> 📘 Câu "indicated". Đoạn 4: `All you have to do is **call us at 555-4933 or send an e-mail** to ... to request an on-site visit` → đặt lịch được bằng **hai cách** ⇒ (B). (A) sai vì công ty phục vụ cả `**office**, bathroom, or **reception area**`; giá được quảng cáo là `some of the best in town` chứ không phải đang **giảm giá** (loại D).

**182.** In the brochure, the word "rough" in paragraph 4, line 2, is closest in meaning to
- (A) **forceful** *(mạnh mẽ, quyết liệt)*
- (B) ✓ **general** *(chung chung, sơ bộ)*
- (C) **coarse** *(thô ráp)*
- (D) **uneven** *(gồ ghề, không đều)*

> 📘 Câu từ vựng theo ngữ cảnh: `visit your space to get **rough measurements**` = lấy số đo **sơ bộ, áng chừng** ⇒ `general`. (C), (D) là nghĩa "thô ráp/gồ ghề" của `rough` — đúng từ nhưng sai ngữ cảnh đo đạc.

**183.** According to the brochure, what information will NOT be provided during an on-site visit?
- (A) ✓ The date of a delivery
- (B) **Descriptions** of materials *(phần mô tả)*
- (C) Prices of products
- (D) The **duration** of a project *(thời lượng)*

> 📘 Câu NOT — đối chiếu đoạn 4: (B) có ở `He will also **describe the various materials** we sell`; (C) có ở `provide you with **cost estimates**`; (D) có ở `let you know **how long it will take**`. Riêng **ngày giao hàng** thì không nằm trong buổi khảo sát — đó chính là điều bà Thomson **phải viết e-mail hỏi thêm** ⇒ (A).

**184.** How much will Ms. Thomson have to pay for the new countertops?
- (A) $90 per square meter
- (B) $120 per square meter
- (C) $150 per square meter
- (D) ✓ $190 per square meter

> 💡 Phải ghép **hai dữ kiện**: (1) e-mail cho biết vợ chồng bà chọn `the **marble** option`; (2) bà cũng xác nhận đây là hàng `**custom** countertops ... need to be **specially ordered**`. Tra bảng giá: hàng **đặt riêng** bằng `granite or **marble**` có giá `**$190 per square meter**` ⇒ (D). Bẫy: $150 là giá marble **loại thường**, còn $120 là giá đặt riêng của laminate/tile.

**185.** What most likely did Ms. Thomson do before Mr. Carlson's visit to her home?
- (A) **Discarded** an old countertop *(vứt bỏ)*
- (B) ✓ Looked through an **informational leaflet** *(tờ rơi thông tin)*
- (C) E-mailed **size specifications** for an installation *(thông số kích thước)*
- (D) Visited a **branch** for a free **consultation** *(chi nhánh / buổi tư vấn)*

> 📘 Câu suy luận. E-mail đoạn 1: `I appreciate the information you provided as it **confirmed what your brochure said**` → bà đã **đọc tờ gấp quảng cáo** từ trước rồi mới đối chiếu được ⇒ (B) (`brochure` = `informational leaflet`). Số đo là do **ông Carlson** đến tận nơi lấy (loại C).

### Questions 186–190 · article, e-mail, and ticket

> **The Regency Scotia School of Design Hosts Historic Royal Costume Display**
>
> *Threads of Royal History*, **launched** on Friday *(khai mạc, ra mắt)*, is the latest **exhibit** from the Regency Scotia School of Design (RSSD) *(cuộc triển lãm)* in Glasgow. It is a **treat** for **period costume enthusiasts** *(món quà thú vị / người mê trang phục cổ)*. For the exhibit, the school borrowed 52 **outfits** *(bộ trang phục)* belonging to both historical and current **monarchs** *(quân vương, hoàng gia)* from museums around the world. **Of particular note** are *(đặc biệt đáng chú ý)* the 4,000 year old **headdress** and accessories *(mũ đội đầu)* **on loan from** the Cairo Museum in Egypt *(được cho mượn từ)* as well as several **stunning robes** *(áo choàng lộng lẫy)* from the Forbidden City Museum in Beijing. In addition, fashion students from the school have created 38 **replica** outfits *(bản sao mô phỏng)*. All of the items are beautifully displayed on specially designed **mannequins** *(ma-nơ-canh)*.
>
> The exhibit will last for a month, **concluding** on April 30 *(kết thúc)*. Regular **admission** is £6.50 *(vé vào cửa)*, but students and **faculty** of the RSSD *(giảng viên)* are admitted for free. **Senior citizens** pay £5.00 *(người cao tuổi)*, and children 10 and under pay £3.50. **Inquire about student group rates** *(hỏi về giá vé đoàn học sinh)* by calling 555-3400. The exhibit space is located in the school's Leighton Hall, which is open from 10 a.m. through 8 p.m., Monday through Thursday.

> TO: Larry Dent <larden@terrington.edu.uk>

>
> Hi Larry,
>
> I saw an article in the paper about RSSD's current exhibit and am thinking of taking the students in my morning art classes there for a **field trip** *(chuyến đi thực tế)*. I called to find out about student group rates, and if there are 30 or more students, the admission fee is **significantly lowered** to £4.00 *(giảm đáng kể)*. But I only have 24 in total for both my morning classes, so I was wondering if you and your students might be interested in joining us. The community center's bus **seats** 55 people *(có chỗ ngồi cho)*, so there should be enough room to **accommodate** everyone *(chứa, đủ chỗ cho)*. It sounds like a really interesting display, and I think our students would learn a lot from it as well. There is **no rush** *(không vội)*, but let me know whether you're interested by Friday so I can **book the visit** *(đặt lịch tham quan)*.
>
> Thanks!
> Rhoda

>

> |---|---|

> All visitors are required to **proceed through the security checkpoint** *(đi qua trạm kiểm tra an ninh)* prior to admission. Photography is **strictly forbidden** *(nghiêm cấm)*. Please do not touch any item **on display** *(đang trưng bày)*.

**186.** What is NOT true about *Threads of Royal History*?
- (A) It includes items **on loan** *(được cho mượn)*.
- (B) It has **pieces** from around the world *(hiện vật)*.
- (C) ✓ It is open to the public on weekends.
- (D) It will close at the end of April.

> 📘 Câu NOT — đối chiếu bài báo: (A) có ở `the ... headdress and accessories **on loan from the Cairo Museum**`; (B) có ở `borrowed 52 outfits ... from museums **around the world**`; (D) có ở `concluding on **April 30**`. Nhưng giờ mở cửa là `**Monday through Thursday**` ⇒ **không** mở cuối tuần ⇒ (C).

**187.** Why did Ms. Augustine want Mr. Dent's students to join her group?
- (A) So that her group becomes **eligible for** a free tour guide *(đủ điều kiện hưởng)*
- (B) ✓ So that she can obtain a discount on admission fees
- (C) So that the school can cover the **expense** of a bus rental *(chi phí)*
- (D) So that her students can **enter their designs into a competition** *(dự thi thiết kế)*

> 📘 E-mail: `if there are **30 or more students**, the admission fee is significantly **lowered to £4.00**. But I only have **24** in total ... so I was wondering if you and your students might be interested in joining us` → bà cần đủ 30 em để **được giá vé đoàn rẻ hơn** ⇒ (B). Xe buýt của trung tâm cộng đồng vốn đã đủ 55 chỗ, không phải vấn đề chi phí (loại C).

**188.** How much were the Terrington Secondary School students charged for admission?
- (A) £3.50 per person
- (B) ✓ £4.00 per person
- (C) £5.00 per person
- (D) £6.50 per person

> 💡 Ghép **e-mail + vé**: e-mail nói `if there are **30 or more** students, the admission fee is lowered to **£4.00**`; vé ghi `Rate: **Student group rate**` với `Participants: **48**` (≥ 30) ⇒ mỗi em trả **£4.00** ⇒ (B). Bẫy: £6.50 là vé thường, £5.00 dành cho **người cao tuổi**, £3.50 cho **trẻ dưới 10 tuổi**.

**189.** What did Mr. Dent's students most likely do on April 28?
- (A) ✓ Viewed **ancient accessories** *(phụ kiện cổ)*
- (B) Toured some local **historic sites** *(di tích lịch sử)*
- (C) Attended a fashion show
- (D) Displayed some outfits they had created

> 💡 Ghép **vé + bài báo**: vé ghi ngày `**April 28**`, do `Rhoda Augustine, **Larry Dent**` dẫn đoàn đi xem *Threads of Royal History*; bài báo cho biết triển lãm trưng bày `the **4,000 year old headdress and accessories** on loan from the Cairo Museum` ⇒ học sinh của ông Dent đã xem các **phụ kiện cổ** ⇒ (A). Bộ trang phục mô phỏng là do sinh viên **RSSD** làm, không phải học sinh trường Terrington (loại D).

**190.** What are visitors to the exhibit asked to do?
- (A) Speak in low voices
- (B) Leave food and drinks outside
- (C) ✓ **Avoid taking pictures** *(tránh chụp ảnh)*
- (D) Reserve a tour guide in advance

> 📘 Cuối vé: `**Photography is strictly forbidden.**` ⇒ khách không được chụp ảnh ⇒ (C). Yêu cầu còn lại là qua trạm an ninh và không sờ hiện vật — không nhắc gì đến đồ ăn thức uống hay hướng dẫn viên.

### Questions 191–195 · flyer, review, and e-mail

> Wheel-Pals, the world's most popular **carpooling** program *(đi chung xe)*, has arrived in Auckland! Download the application today onto your mobile phone, **device**, or computer *(thiết bị)*, and find out how easy it is to save money on transportation! The application connects you with other users in your area that are interested in carpooling at specific times. All our members **undergo a screening process** *(trải qua quy trình sàng lọc, xét duyệt)* and can post reviews about other passengers and drivers. With **fees** starting as low as $6 per month *(phí)* for a **basic package** *(gói cơ bản)*, Wheel-Pals is sure to be as popular here in Auckland as it is in Brisbane, Sydney, and Melbourne! Go to www.wheelpals.com for more details.

> **USER REVIEW: Wheel-Pals Carpooling Application**
> By Jason Diaz (jdiaz@vastpost.co.au)
>
> Wheel-Pals recently **launched** its service *(ra mắt)* in the Auckland area, and having already used the application while living in Brisbane, I was one of the first to **sign up** *(đăng ký)*. As before, I purchased the basic package because, in the past, it had enough services to help me **form carpool groups** *(lập nhóm đi chung xe)* with other drivers or **riders** that live nearby *(người đi nhờ xe)*. However, I don't think Wheel-Pals has done enough to **promote its launch** *(quảng bá cho việc ra mắt)* in this area. I sometimes find it difficult to find people to carpool with. In Brisbane, I only drove my car to work once or twice a week, but now I have to do it more often. Also, few people that I know locally are even **aware of** the application *(biết đến)*.

Bản trên đã khôi phục và **đối chiếu khớp từng câu** với bản dịch chính thức (KEY trang 33–34), gồm cả địa chỉ `asimmonds@**wheelpals**.co.au`.

**191.** What is the purpose of the flyer?
- (A) ✓ To **publicize** availability of a software application *(quảng bá, thông báo rộng rãi)*
- (B) To announce **price reductions** for a **specified period** *(giảm giá / khoảng thời gian nhất định)*
- (C) To request reviews from users of a **ride-share** program *(đi chung xe)*
- (D) To inform **subscribers** of special services available *(người đăng ký thuê bao)*

> 📘 Tờ rơi mở đầu `Wheel-Pals ... **has arrived in Auckland**! **Download the application today**` → giới thiệu rằng ứng dụng nay đã có mặt tại Auckland ⇒ (A). Mức `$6 per month` là **giá khởi điểm**, không phải đợt giảm giá có thời hạn (loại B).

**192.** What problem does Mr. Diaz mention in the review?
- (A) People find a program **complicated** to use *(phức tạp)*.
- (B) Membership fees are too high.
- (C) ✓ Local residents are **unfamiliar with** a program *(chưa quen, chưa biết đến)*.
- (D) Drivers **take on** too many passengers *(nhận chở)*.

> 📘 Cuối bài đánh giá: `I don't think Wheel-Pals has done enough to promote its launch in this area` và `**few people that I know locally are even aware of the application**` → dân địa phương **chưa biết tới** ứng dụng ⇒ (C). Ông không phàn nàn về giá hay độ khó sử dụng.

**193.** What is indicated about Mr. Diaz?
- (A) He has never used Wheel-Pals before.
- (B) He drives his car up to twice a week.
- (C) He recently **relocated to** Brisbane *(chuyển đến)*.
- (D) ✓ He pays six dollars per month for his subscription.

> 💡 Ghép **tờ rơi + bài đánh giá**: tờ rơi ghi `fees starting as low as **$6 per month** for a **basic package**`; bài đánh giá viết `I purchased the **basic package**` ⇒ ông trả **6 đô/tháng** ⇒ (D). (A) trái với `having already used the application while living in Brisbane`; (B) là thói quen **hồi ở Brisbane** — nay ông phải lái `**more often**`; (C) ngược chiều: ông **rời** Brisbane để tới Auckland.

**194.** Where does Wheel-Pals plan to do additional product promotion?
- (A) In Brisbane
- (B) In Sydney
- (C) In Melbourne
- (D) ✓ In Auckland

> 💡 Ghép **bài đánh giá + e-mail**: e-mail hứa `take steps to market the program further **in your area**`; bài đánh giá cho biết ông Diaz đang ở **Auckland** (`Wheel-Pals recently launched its service in the **Auckland** area ... I was one of the first to sign up`) ⇒ nơi sẽ được quảng bá thêm là **Auckland** ⇒ (D). Brisbane, Sydney, Melbourne là những nơi ứng dụng **đã phổ biến sẵn**.

**195.** In the e-mail, the word "leave" in paragraph 1, line 3, is closest in meaning to
- (A) **exit** *(rời khỏi)*
- (B) ✓ **make** *(đưa ra, để lại (lời nhận xét))*
- (C) **remain** *(ở lại, còn lại)*
- (D) **assign** *(giao, phân công)*

> 📘 Câu từ vựng theo ngữ cảnh: `thank you for taking the time to **leave the comment**` = cảm ơn vì đã **để lại/đưa ra** lời nhận xét ⇒ `make` (make a comment). (A), (C) là các nghĩa "rời đi / còn lại" của `leave` — sai ngữ cảnh.

>
> **VENUE RENTAL:** Let us **host your gathering** *(tổ chức buổi họp mặt của bạn)* in our private **function room** *(phòng tổ chức sự kiện)*! We recently **made a deal to partner with** a manufacturing company *(ký thoả thuận hợp tác với)* in India, and a team of their **representatives** *(đại diện)* are going to arrive next week to **finalize our agreement** *(hoàn tất thoả thuận)*. > Spartan Tires announced in a **press statement** yesterday *(thông cáo báo chí)* that it will finalize a deal with India-based **manufacturer** Maha-Rubber *(nhà sản xuất)* on June 18. The company will produce tires under the Spartan Tires brand. Spartan Tires has 38 **branches** across the country *(chi nhánh)*, with an average of four new **franchises** opening **annually** *(cửa hàng nhượng quyền / hằng năm)*. However, it does not produce its own **accessories or parts** *(phụ kiện hay linh kiện)*, **carrying** only those made by other companies *(bày bán)*. Spartan Tires CEO Carlo Gregario said in the statement, "We don't have the **facilities** to produce our own tires *(cơ sở sản xuất)*. Partnering with Maha-Rubber will allow us to do so at a **reasonable cost** *(chi phí hợp lý)*." **196.** What is NOT true about Coriander Fine Dining?

Nhưng giao hàng chỉ miễn phí `for orders **over $80**` — đơn nhỏ hơn vẫn bị tính `a $5 delivery fee` ⇒ (C) sai vì chữ **any order**.

**197.** What does Mr. Potter ask about?
- (A) The availability of an event **venue** *(địa điểm)*
- (B) ✓ The price for catering a dinner
- (C) The date of a final agreement
- (D) The cost of renting out parking

Bữa ăn tổ chức ngay tại `**here at our headquarters**` nên ông không hỏi thuê phòng của nhà hàng (loại A); ngày ký hợp đồng thì chính ông đã biết (loại C).

**198.** What will Mr. Potter have to pay extra for?

- (C) Getting food delivered outside the city
- (D) Serving an **assortment of appetizers** *(nhiều loại món khai vị)*

> 💡 Ghép **trang dịch vụ + e-mail**: trang dịch vụ ghi `Service dishes, plates, **cutlery**, and glassware are also available for an **additional charge**`; e-mail của ông viết `We **do not have any dishes or dining ware**, so we will need those items as well` ⇒ ông phải trả thêm cho bộ đồ ăn ⇒ (A). Bữa tiệc dùng dịch vụ **catering tại trụ sở của ông**, không phải giao hàng, nên (C) không liên quan.

**199.** In the article, the word "carrying" in paragraph 1, line 4, is closest in meaning to
- (A) **enduring** *(chịu đựng)*
- (B) **transporting** *(vận chuyển)*
- (C) ✓ **retailing** *(bán lẻ, bày bán)*
- (D) **absorbing** *(hấp thụ)*

> 📘 Câu từ vựng theo ngữ cảnh: `it does not produce its own accessories or parts, **carrying** only those made by other companies` = hãng chỉ **bày bán** hàng do công ty khác làm ⇒ `retailing`. (B) là nghĩa "chuyên chở" của `carry` — đúng từ nhưng sai ngữ cảnh cửa hàng.

**200.** Who might Ms. Shiraz be preparing a meal for on June 18?
- (A) **Retiring executives** at Spartan Tires *(lãnh đạo sắp nghỉ hưu)*
- (B) ✓ Visitors from Maha-Rubber
- (C) Guests at an event held at Coriander Fine Dining
- (D) Participants in a celebration **hosted by** Lucas Industries *(do ... tổ chức)*

> 💡 Ghép **e-mail + bài báo**: e-mail đặt bữa tối ngày `**June 18**` cho đoàn `representatives` của `a manufacturing company **in India**` sang ký hợp đồng; bài báo cho biết đối tác Ấn Độ đó chính là `India-based manufacturer **Maha-Rubber**`, và thoả thuận được chốt đúng ngày `**June 18**` ⇒ bà Shiraz nấu cho **khách từ Maha-Rubber** ⇒ (B). Bữa ăn diễn ra tại **trụ sở Spartan Tires** (loại C); tiệc của Lucas Industries là sự kiện **đã qua** (loại D).

### Questions 147–148 · flyer

> **Green Wave Consulting Services**
> Saving the planet while saving you money!
>
> We all know that we need to reduce the amount of **greenhouse gases** *(khí nhà kính)* being **released into the atmosphere** *(thải vào khí quyển)*. But few of us realize that our own **residences** are often **to blame** *(nơi ở / là nguyên nhân, đáng trách)*. Homes are the third largest source of **emissions** in the entire country *(khí thải)*! Luckily, there's now **a whole host of** easy solutions *(cả loạt)* to reduce your **environmental impact** *(tác động tới môi trường)*. And the best thing of all is that doing so can save you money as well.
>
> Our money-saving green changes include:
> • Installing wall **insulation** *(lớp cách nhiệt)*
> • Fitting homes with new, **efficient water heaters** *(máy nước nóng tiết kiệm năng lượng)*
> • Switching to **energy-saving lightbulbs** *(bóng đèn tiết kiệm điện)*
> • Putting in **window coverings** that keep heat in *(rèm/lớp che cửa sổ)*
>
> Call 535-9974 today to **arrange for** one of our **technicians** *(thu xếp / kỹ thuật viên)* to visit you at home for a **consultation** *(buổi tư vấn)*. It's completely free, and we'll **get back to you** *(phản hồi lại)* within three **business days** *(ngày làm việc)* with a detailed personal plan for making your home more **energy efficient** *(tiết kiệm năng lượng)*.
>
> For any other **inquiries** *(thắc mắc)*, visit our offices on 593 Rayburn Street in downtown Portland, Monday through Saturday from 10 a.m. to 7 p.m.

**147.** What is mentioned about greenhouse gas emissions?
- (A) They recently went up by a large amount.
- (B) ✓ Residences are one source of them.
- (C) They are limited by the government.
- (D) **Meters** can be installed to measure them *(đồng hồ đo)*.

> 📘 Câu "mentioned". Đoạn 1: `our own **residences** are often to blame. **Homes are the third largest source of emissions** in the entire country` → nhà ở là **một trong những nguồn** phát thải ⇒ (B). Tờ rơi không nói về mức tăng, quy định của chính phủ hay thiết bị đo.

**148.** How can customers receive a **customized** energy saving plan? *(được thiết kế riêng)*
- (A) ✓ By arranging a meeting with a **consultant** *(chuyên viên tư vấn)*
- (B) By **inputting** their details on a Web site *(nhập)*
- (C) By visiting the Green Wave **headquarters** *(trụ sở chính)*
- (D) By sending an e-mail to a **specialist** *(chuyên gia)*

> 📘 Đoạn 3: `**Call 535-9974** today to arrange for one of our technicians to **visit you at home for a consultation** ... we'll get back to you ... with a **detailed personal plan**` → gọi điện hẹn kỹ thuật viên đến tận nhà ⇒ (A). Việc đến văn phòng chỉ dành cho `any **other** inquiries` (loại C).

### Questions 149–150 · text-message chain

> **Marge Bledsoe** [11:05 a.m.]
> I just remembered I have a client who'd like to see the house that we've **listed** on 78 Crescent Lane *(đưa vào danh sách rao bán)*. Unfortunately, I'm **fully booked** today *(kín lịch)*. Is anyone free this afternoon?
>
> **Evan Clark** [11:08 A.M.]
> I can bring the client to the house at 2:30. I have another **showing** nearby *(buổi dẫn khách xem nhà)* and can go there **afterward** *(sau đó)*.
>
> **Julia Anderson** [11:14 A.M.]
> Marge, are you **referring to** Eva Teal *(nói đến)*? She called a short while ago and said she was looking for a two-bedroom place downtown.
>
> **Marge Bledsoe** [11:15 a.m.]
> Yes, that's her. I think Evan can **handle** it *(lo được)*. I need your help with another client, Julia.
>
> **Julia Anderson** [11:15 a.m.]
> Sure, just let me know later.
>
> **Evan Clark** [11:17 a.m.]
> OK. Why don't I show her the other home nearby as well? It's just a few **blocks** away from there *(dãy phố)* and also has two bedrooms.
>
> **Marge Bledsoe** [11:18 a.m.]
> Good call. Will find out if she's interested in seeing it. I'll give her your mobile number **in case** there's a problem *(phòng khi)*. She can probably meet you there first before seeing the Crescent Lane house.

**149.** Who most likely is Ms. Anderson?
- (A) A building **administrator** *(người quản lý toà nhà)*
- (B) ✓ A **real estate agent** *(nhân viên môi giới bất động sản)*
- (C) A housing **inspector** *(thanh tra viên)*
- (D) An office **receptionist** *(lễ tân)*

> 📘 Câu suy luận nghề nghiệp. Bà Anderson nhận điện của khách tìm nhà (`She called ... said she was looking for a **two-bedroom place downtown**`), gọi họ là "client", và bà Bledsoe nhờ bà `help with **another client**` — cả nhóm đều **rao bán và dẫn khách xem nhà** ⇒ họ là môi giới bất động sản ⇒ (B).

**150.** At 11:18 a.m., what does Ms. Bledsoe mean when she writes, "Good call"?
- (A) She can confirm that Mr. Clark has the right information.
- (B) She is **relieved** to find out that someone can replace her *(nhẹ nhõm)*.
- (C) She is pleased with the results of a phone conversation.
- (D) ✓ She likes the **proposal** that Mr. Clark has made *(đề xuất)*.

> 📘 Câu hỏi ý định. Ngay trước đó ông Clark đề xuất `**Why don't I show her the other home nearby as well?**`; bà đáp "Good call" rồi hành động theo (`Will find out if she's interested in seeing it`) → bà **tán thành đề xuất** đó ⇒ (D). Ở đây `call` mang nghĩa "**quyết định/ý kiến**", không phải "cuộc gọi" — bẫy của phương án (C).

And as the storm travels, other tour **destinations** may be affected as well *(điểm đến)*.

If you choose this option, we recommend waiting at least until May so that your visit to the different locations is as **pleasant** as possible *(dễ chịu)*.
